     1→# dotnix macOS (Darwin) 対応計画
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→dotnix は現在 NixOS (x86_64-linux) 専用。macOS (aarch64-darwin) にも対応させ、1つのリポジトリで両環境を管理する。
     6→
     7→- **参考**: ryoppippi/dotfiles のモジュール分離パターン（`home/`, `darwin/`, `linux/`）
     8→- **移植元**: learn-nix リポジトリの nix-darwin 設定、Homebrew統合、1Password Shell Plugins
     9→- **既存 NixOS 設定は一切壊さない**
    10→
    11→---
    12→
    13→## Step 1: flake.nix の拡張
    14→
    15→**ファイル**: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix`
    16→
    17→### 1a. inputs 追加
    18→
    19→```nix
    20→nix-darwin = {
    21→  url = "github:LnL7/nix-darwin";
    22→  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    23→};
    24→_1password-shell-plugins.url = "github:1Password/shell-plugins";
    25→```
    26→
    27→### 1b. system ハードコード解消 + externalOverlay の動的化
    28→
    29→`let system = "x86_64-linux"` を削除。`externalOverlay` 内で `final.system` を使う。
    30→ghostty は Darwin でパッケージ未提供なので条件分岐。
    31→
    32→```nix
    33→externalOverlay = final: prev: {
    34→  claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.claude-code;
    35→  ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.ccusage;
    36→  codex = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.codex;
    37→  opencode = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.opencode;
    38→  vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.vibe-kanban;
    39→  version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${final.system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
    40→    doCheck = false;
    41→  });
    42→  gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${final.system}.default;
    43→} // (if final.stdenv.isLinux then {
    44→  ghostty = ghostty.packages.${final.system}.default;
    45→} else {});
    46→```
    47→
    48→### 1c. dotfilesDir を引数化
    49→
    50→```nix
    51→mkDotfilesDir = homeDir: "${homeDir}/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix";
    52→```
    53→
    54→### 1d. mkDarwin 関数と darwinConfigurations 追加
    55→
    56→```nix
    57→mkDarwin =
    58→  {
    59→    host,
    60→    system,
    61→    extraModules ? [],
    62→  }:
    63→  let
    64→    dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/Users/yuta";
    65→  in
    66→  nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
    67→    inherit system;
    68→    specialArgs = {
    69→      inherit helpers dotfilesDir home-manager local-skills
    70→              anthropic-skills vercel-skills ui-ux-pro-max-skill
    71→              agent-skills _1password-shell-plugins;
    72→    };
    73→    modules = [
    74→      ./nix/modules/darwin
    75→      ./nix/hosts/${host}
    76→      ./nix/profiles/darwin.nix
    77→      {
    78→        nixpkgs.overlays = [ externalOverlay moonbit-overlay.overlays.default customOverlay ];
    79→        nixpkgs.config.allowUnfreePredicate =
    80→          pkg: builtins.elem (nixpkgs.lib.getName pkg) [
    81→            "claude-code" "vscode" "discord" "slack"
    82→            "obsidian" "1password" "spotify"
    83→          ];
    84→      }
    85→    ] ++ extraModules;
    86→  };
    87→```
    88→
    89→outputs に追加:
    90→```nix
    91→darwinConfigurations.darwin = mkDarwin {
    92→  host = "darwin";
    93→  system = "aarch64-darwin";
    94→};
    95→```
    96→
    97→既存の `mkSystem` は `dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/home/yuta"` に変更するだけで、ロジックはそのまま。
    98→
    99→### 1e. description 更新
   100→
   101→```nix
   102→description = "yuta's NixOS & macOS configuration";
   103→```
   104→
   105→---
   106→
   107→## Step 2: Darwin モジュール新設
   108→
   109→### 2a. `nix/modules/darwin/default.nix` (新規)
   110→
   111→```nix

   122→

   124→
   125→learn-nix の `nix/nix-darwin/default.nix` から移植。Modern Installer（公式 nix-installer）を使うため、`nix.enable` はデフォルト（true）のまま nix-darwin に管理を任せる。
   126→
   127→- `system.stateVersion = 6`
   128→- `nix.settings.experimental-features = [ "nix-command" "flakes" ]`
   129→- macOS defaults: Dock, Finder, screencapture, trackpad, LaunchServices
   130→- `security.pam.services.sudo_local.touchIdAuth = true` (Touch ID for sudo)
   131→
   132→### 2c. `nix/modules/darwin/homebrew.nix` (新規)
   133→
   134→macOS GUI アプリは Homebrew cask で管理（Nix パッケージが提供されないものが多い）。
   135→
   136→```nix
   137→homebrew = {
   138→  enable = true;
   139→  onActivation.autoUpdate = true;
   140→  casks = [
   141→    "ghostty" "raycast" "google-chrome" "discord" "slack"
   142→    "obsidian" "1password" "spotify" "android-studio"
   143→  ];

   146→
   147→### 2d. `nix/modules/darwin/fonts.nix` (新規)
   148→
   149→`nix/modules/core/fonts.nix` と同等のフォントを `fonts.packages` で設定。

   151→### 2e. `nix/modules/darwin/user.nix` (新規)
   152→
   153→```nix
   154→{ pkgs, home-manager, helpers, dotfilesDir, local-skills,
   155→  anthropic-skills, vercel-skills, ui-ux-pro-max-skill,
   156→  agent-skills, _1password-shell-plugins, ... }:
   157→{
   158→  imports = [ home-manager.darwinModules.home-manager ];
   159→
   160→  home-manager = {
   161→    useGlobalPkgs = true;
   162→    useUserPackages = true;
   163→    extraSpecialArgs = {

   166→              _1password-shell-plugins;
   167→    };
   168→    sharedModules = [ agent-skills.homeManagerModules.default ];
   169→    users.yuta = import ../home/darwin.nix;
   170→  };
   171→
   172→  users.users.yuta.home = "/Users/yuta";
   173→  nix.settings.trusted-users = [ "root" "yuta" ];
   174→}
   175→```

   179→## Step 3: Darwin ホスト設定
   180→

   182→
   183→```nix

   228→

   258→残すもの: claude-code, ccusage, codex, opencode, vibe-kanban, aqua, git, git-wt, lazygit, jujutsu, jj-desc, entire, moonbit-bin, nil, nixfmt-tree, nix-init, google-cloud-sdk, nodejs_22, bun, ni, curl, wget, aria2, xh, ripgrep, fzf, peco, jq, jnv, tokei, similarity-ts, lsd, btop, zoxide, tree, glow, vhs, zellij, yazi, keifu, speedtest-cli, bandwhich, nmap, dnsutils, sl, fastfetch

   263→

   277→

   300→## Step 7: keifu overlay の Darwin 対応
   301→
   302→**ファイル**: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/overlays/keifu.nix`
   303→
   304→openssl 依存が Darwin では `Security`/`SystemConfiguration` framework を必要とするため、`buildInputs` を分岐する。
   305→
   306→```nix
   307→buildInputs = [
   308→  final.openssl
   309→] ++ final.lib.optionals final.stdenv.isDarwin [
   310→  final.darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.Security
   311→  final.darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.SystemConfiguration
   312→];
   313→```

   317→## Step 8: zsh エイリアスのプラットフォーム分岐
   318→
   319→**ファイル**: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/zsh/config/aliases.zsh`
   320→
   321→```zsh
   322→if [[ "$(uname)" == "Darwin" ]]; then
   323→  alias rebuild="darwin-rebuild switch --flake ~/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix && source ~/.zshrc"
   324→  alias cpd="pwd | pbcopy"
   325→else
   326→  alias rebuild="nh os switch && source ~/.zshrc"
   327→  alias cpd="pwd | wl-copy"
   328→fi
   329→```

   343→## Step 10: CLAUDE.md 更新
   344→
   345→Darwin 用コマンドを追記。
   346→
   347→```markdown

   356→
   357→---
   358→
   359→## 前提条件（初回セットアップ）
   360→
   361→macOS マシンで初めて適用する前に、以下を手動でインストールしておく必要がある:
   362→
   363→1. **Nix (Modern Installer)**: 公式の nix-installer（Determinate Nix Installer のフォーク、NixOS コミュニティ管理）を使用。Flakes 有効化やアンインストールが容易。
   364→   ```bash
   365→   curl -L https://nixos.org/nix/install | sh
   366→   ```
   367→2. **Homebrew**: `homebrew.enable = true` で cask を管理するため、Homebrew がインストール済みであること
   368→   ```bash
   369→   /bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Homebrew/install/HEAD/install.sh)"
   370→   ```
   371→3. **ghq のルートディレクトリ**: `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix` にこのリポジトリが clone されていること
   372→
   373→---
   374→
   375→## 注意事項
   376→
   377→1. **ghostty**: Darwin では flake パッケージ未提供 → Homebrew cask で管理。`externalOverlay` で `isLinux` ガード必須
   378→2. **nix-hazkey**: NixOS モジュール専用。`mkDarwin` には含めない（macOS は別途 IME を用意）
   379→3. **niri**: Wayland 専用。Darwin の `specialArgs` からは除外

## Approved Plan:
# dotnix macOS (Darwin) 対応計画

## Context

dotnix は現在 NixOS (x86_64-linux) 専用。macOS (aarch64-darwin) にも対応させ、1つのリポジトリで両環境を管理する。

- **参考**: ryoppippi/dotfiles のモジュール分離パターン（`home/`, `darwin/`, `linux/`）
- **移植元**: learn-nix リポジトリの nix-darwin 設定、Homebrew統合、1Password Shell Plugins
- **既存 NixOS 設定は一切壊さない**

---

## Step 1: flake.nix の拡張

**ファイル**: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix`

### 1a. inputs 追加

```nix
nix-darwin = {
  url = "github:LnL7/nix-darwin";
  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
};
_1password-shell-plugins.url = "github:1Password/shell-plugins";
```

### 1b. system ハードコード解消 + externalOverlay の動的化

`let system = "x86_64-linux"` を削除。`externalOverlay` 内で `final.system` を使う。
ghostty は Darwin でパッケージ未提供なので条件分岐。

```nix
externalOverlay = final: prev: {
  claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.claude-code;
  ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.ccusage;
  codex = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.codex;
  opencode = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.opencode;
  vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.vibe-kanban;
  version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${final.system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
    doCheck = false;
  });
  gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${final.system}.default;
} // (if final.stdenv.isLinux then {
  ghostty = ghostty.packages.${final.system}.default;
} else {});
```

### 1c. dotfilesDir を引数化

```nix
mkDotfilesDir = homeDir: "${homeDir}/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix";
```

### 1d. mkDarwin 関数と darwinConfigurations 追加

```nix
mkDarwin =
  {
    host,
    system,
    extraModules ? [],
  }:
  let
    dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/Users/yuta";
  in
  nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
    inherit system;
    specialArgs = {
      inherit helpers dotfilesDir home-manager local-skills
              anthropic-skills vercel-skills ui-ux-pro-max-skill
              agent-skills _1password-shell-plugins;
    };
    modules = [
      ./nix/modules/darwin
      ./nix/hosts/${host}
      ./nix/profiles/darwin.nix
      {
        nixpkgs.overlays = [ externalOverlay moonbit-overlay.overlays.default customOverlay ];
        nixpkgs.config.allowUnfreePredicate =
          pkg: builtins.elem (nixpkgs.lib.getName pkg) [
            "claude-code" "vscode" "discord" "slack"
            "obsidian" "1password" "spotify"
          ];
      }
    ] ++ extraModules;
  };
```

outputs に追加:
```nix
darwinConfigurations.darwin = mkDarwin {
  host = "darwin";
  system = "aarch64-darwin";
};
```

既存の `mkSystem` は `dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/home/yuta"` に変更するだけで、ロジックはそのまま。

### 1e. description 更新

```nix
description = "yuta's NixOS & macOS configuration";
```

---

## Step 2: Darwin モジュール新設

### 2a. `nix/modules/darwin/default.nix` (新規)

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  imports = [
    ./system.nix
    ./homebrew.nix
    ./fonts.nix
    ./user.nix
  ];
}
```

### 2b. `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` (新規)

learn-nix の `nix/nix-darwin/default.nix` から移植。Modern Installer（公式 nix-installer）を使うため、`nix.enable` はデフォルト（true）のまま nix-darwin に管理を任せる。

- `system.stateVersion = 6`
- `nix.settings.experimental-features = [ "nix-command" "flakes" ]`
- macOS defaults: Dock, Finder, screencapture, trackpad, LaunchServices
- `security.pam.services.sudo_local.touchIdAuth = true` (Touch ID for sudo)

### 2c. `nix/modules/darwin/homebrew.nix` (新規)

macOS GUI アプリは Homebrew cask で管理（Nix パッケージが提供されないものが多い）。

```nix
homebrew = {
  enable = true;
  onActivation.autoUpdate = true;
  casks = [
    "ghostty" "raycast" "google-chrome" "discord" "slack"
    "obsidian" "1password" "spotify" "android-studio"
  ];
};
```

### 2d. `nix/modules/darwin/fonts.nix` (新規)

`nix/modules/core/fonts.nix` と同等のフォントを `fonts.packages` で設定。

### 2e. `nix/modules/darwin/user.nix` (新規)

```nix
{ pkgs, home-manager, helpers, dotfilesDir, local-skills,
  anthropic-skills, vercel-skills, ui-ux-pro-max-skill,
  agent-skills, _1password-shell-plugins, ... }:
{
  imports = [ home-manager.darwinModules.home-manager ];

  home-manager = {
    useGlobalPkgs = true;
    useUserPackages = true;
    extraSpecialArgs = {
      inherit helpers dotfilesDir local-skills
              anthropic-skills vercel-skills ui-ux-pro-max-skill
              _1password-shell-plugins;
    };
    sharedModules = [ agent-skills.homeManagerModules.default ];
    users.yuta = import ../home/darwin.nix;
  };

  users.users.yuta.home = "/Users/yuta";
  nix.settings.trusted-users = [ "root" "yuta" ];
}
```

---

## Step 3: Darwin ホスト設定

### 3a. `nix/hosts/darwin/default.nix` (新規)

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  networking.hostName = "darwin";
}
```

---

## Step 4: home-manager の分離

### 4a. `nix/modules/home/default.nix` 変更

`home.homeDirectory` のハードコードを削除（各エントリファイルで設定）。

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  imports = [
    ./dotfiles.nix
    ./agent-skills.nix
    ./programs/claude-code.nix
    ./programs/git.nix
    ./programs/jj.nix
    ./programs/zsh.nix
  ];

  home.username = "yuta";
  home.stateVersion = "25.11";
}
```

### 4b. `nix/modules/home/linux.nix` (新規)

NixOS 側のエントリポイント。

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  imports = [ ./default.nix ];
  home.homeDirectory = "/home/yuta";
}
```

### 4c. `nix/modules/home/darwin.nix` (新規)

macOS 側のエントリポイント。1Password Shell Plugins を統合。

```nix
{ pkgs, _1password-shell-plugins, ... }:
{
  imports = [
    ./default.nix
    _1password-shell-plugins.hmModules.default
  ];

  home.homeDirectory = "/Users/yuta";

  programs._1password-shell-plugins = {
    enable = true;
    plugins = with pkgs; [ gh awscli2 ];
  };
}
```

### 4d. `nix/modules/core/user.nix` 変更

`import ./../home` → `import ./../home/linux.nix` に変更。

---

## Step 5: packages.nix のプラットフォーム分離

### 5a. `nix/modules/home/packages.nix` を共通パッケージのみに変更

残すもの: claude-code, ccusage, codex, opencode, vibe-kanban, aqua, git, git-wt, lazygit, jujutsu, jj-desc, entire, moonbit-bin, nil, nixfmt-tree, nix-init, google-cloud-sdk, nodejs_22, bun, ni, curl, wget, aria2, xh, ripgrep, fzf, peco, jq, jnv, tokei, similarity-ts, lsd, btop, zoxide, tree, glow, vhs, zellij, yazi, keifu, speedtest-cli, bandwhich, nmap, dnsutils, sl, fastfetch

### 5b. `nix/modules/home/packages-linux.nix` (新規)

Linux 固有を移動: ghostty, android-tools, android-studio, yubikey-manager, yubioath-flutter, pam_u2f, pamtester, google-chrome, discord, slack, vscode, obsidian, anki, \_1password-gui, insomnia, spotify, rofi, cliphist, wl-clipboard, swww, grimblast, swappy, zenity, hyprlock, brightnessctl, rpi-imager, difit, binutils, arp-scan, cava, vrcx

### 5c. `nix/modules/home/packages-darwin.nix` (新規)

macOS 固有の Nix パッケージ（GUI アプリは homebrew.nix で管理するのでここは最小限）。

### 5d. profiles の import 調整

`cli.nix`, `cli-server.nix` で `packages-linux.nix` を追加 import。

## Step 6: Darwin プロファイル新設

### 6a. `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` (新規)

```nix
{ ... }:
{
  home-manager.users.yuta.imports = [
    ./../modules/home/packages.nix
    ./../modules/home/packages-darwin.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/gh.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/tmux
    ./../modules/home/programs/neovim.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/bat.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/btop.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/fastfetch
    ./../modules/home/programs/vscode.nix
    ./../modules/home/programs/ghostty
  ];
}
```

flake.nix の `mkDarwin` で darwin プロファイルを直接 module として読み込む。

---

## Step 7: keifu overlay の Darwin 対応

**ファイル**: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/overlays/keifu.nix`

openssl 依存が Darwin では `Security`/`SystemConfiguration` framework を必要とするため、`buildInputs` を分岐する。

```nix
buildInputs = [
  final.openssl
] ++ final.lib.optionals final.stdenv.isDarwin [
  final.darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.Security
  final.darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.SystemConfiguration
];
```

---

## Step 8: zsh エイリアスのプラットフォーム分岐

**ファイル**: `/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/zsh/config/aliases.zsh`

```zsh
if [[ "$(uname)" == "Darwin" ]]; then
  alias rebuild="darwin-rebuild switch --flake ~/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix && source ~/.zshrc"
  alias cpd="pwd | pbcopy"
else
  alias rebuild="nh os switch && source ~/.zshrc"
  alias cpd="pwd | wl-copy"
fi
```

---

learn-nix の `raycast/` ディレクトリを dotnix ルートにコピー。

- `raycast/append-memos.sh`
- `raycast/discord.sh`
- `raycast/jisho_lookup.sh`

## Step 10: CLAUDE.md 更新

Darwin 用コマンドを追記。

```markdown
## Commands

### NixOS
nh os switch

### macOS
darwin-rebuild switch --flake .
```

---

## 前提条件（初回セットアップ）

macOS マシンで初めて適用する前に、以下を手動でインストールしておく必要がある:

1. **Nix (Modern Installer)**: 公式の nix-installer（Determinate Nix Installer のフォーク、NixOS コミュニティ管理）を使用。Flakes 有効化やアンインストールが容易。
   ```bash
   curl -L https://nixos.org/nix/install | sh
   ```
2. **Homebrew**: `homebrew.enable = true` で cask を管理するため、Homebrew がインストール済みであること
   ```bash
   /bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Homebrew/install/HEAD/install.sh)"
   ```
3. **ghq のルートディレクトリ**: `/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix` にこのリポジトリが clone されていること

---

## 注意事項

1. **ghostty**: Darwin では flake パッケージ未提供 → Homebrew cask で管理。`externalOverlay` で `isLinux` ガード必須
2. **nix-hazkey**: NixOS モジュール専用。`mkDarwin` には含めない（macOS は別途 IME を用意）
3. **niri**: Wayland 専用。Darwin の `specialArgs` からは除外
4. **keifu overlay の Darwin 対応**: `keifu.nix` は openssl に依存しており、Darwin では `darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.Security` が追加で必要。overlay 内で `final.stdenv.isDarwin` を使い `buildInputs` を分岐する
   ```nix
   buildInputs = [
     final.openssl
   ] ++ final.lib.optionals final.stdenv.isDarwin [
     final.darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.Security
     final.darwin.apple_sdk.frameworks.SystemConfiguration
   ];
   ```
5. **dotfilesDir**: macOS では `/Users/yuta/ghq/...`。ghq のデフォルトルートが同じ構造である前提

---

## 検証手順

1. `nix flake check` で flake の整合性確認（NixOS と Darwin 両方）
2. NixOS マシンで `nh os switch` して既存環境が壊れないことを確認
3. macOS マシンで `darwin-rebuild switch --flake .#darwin` を実行
4. macOS で基本ツール（zsh, git, neovim, tmux, claude-code 等）が動作することを確認
5. macOS で Homebrew cask アプリ（Ghostty, Raycast 等）がインストールされることを確認

---

     1→# CLAUDE.md
     2→
     3→This file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.
     4→
     5→## Commands
     6→
     7→```bash
     8→# システム設定を反映（rebuild）
     9→nh os switch
    10→# または
    11→sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .
    12→
    13→# 特定のパッケージを検索
    14→nix search nixpkgs <package>
    15→
    16→# flake入力を更新
    17→nix flake update
    18→```
    19→
    20→## Agent Skills
    21→
    22→このリポジトリは`agent-skills-nix`でスキルを管理しています。
    23→
    24→- **設定**: `nix/modules/home/agent-skills.nix`
    25→- **ローカルスキル**: `agents/skills/`
    26→- **外部スキル**: `anthropics/skills`, `vercel-labs/skills`
    27→- **デプロイ先**: `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.config/claude/skills`
    28→
    29→主なスキル：
    30→- `social-digest` - Discord + Mastodon投稿をObsidianに保存（ローカル）
    31→- `oura-daily-watch` - Oura Ring データ + Discord行動分析（ローカル）
    32→- `docx`, `pdf`, `pptx`, `xlsx` - ドキュメント処理（Anthropic）
    33→- `frontend-design`, `skill-creator`, `webapp-testing` - 開発支援（Anthropic）
    34→- `find-skills` - スキル検索・発見支援（Vercel）
    35→- `ui-ux-pro-max` - UI/UXデザインシステム生成（コミュニティ）
    36→
    37→詳細: `agents/README.md`
    38→
    39→## Architecture
    40→
    41→NixOS flake構成 with home-manager（nixos-unstable）
    42→
    43→```
    44→flake.nix                          # エントリポイント
    45→├── agents/
    46→│   └── skills/                    # Claude Codeスキル
    47→├── nix/
    48→│   ├── hosts/                     # ホスト固有の設定
    49→│   │   └── nixos/
    50→│   │       ├── default.nix        # システム基本設定（boot, network, locale）
    51→│   │       └── hardware-configuration.nix
    52→│   ├── profiles/                  # プロファイル定義
    53→│   │   ├── cli-minimal.nix        # 最小CLI環境
    54→│   │   ├── cli.nix                # CLI環境（docker, tailscale含む）
    55→│   │   └── gui.nix                # GUI環境（niri, audio, bluetooth含む）
    56→│   ├── modules/
    57→│   │   ├── core/                  # システムモジュール
    58→│   │   │   ├── default.nix
    59→│   │   │   ├── packages.nix       # システムパッケージ（firefox, zsh, nh, nix-ld）
    60→│   │   │   ├── user.nix           # ユーザー設定 + home-manager統合
    61→│   │   │   ├── niri.nix           # Niri WM + greetd
    62→│   │   │   ├── input.nix          # fcitx5 + hazkey
    63→│   │   │   ├── audio.nix          # pipewire
    64→│   │   │   ├── bluetooth.nix
    65→│   │   │   ├── pam.nix            # PAM/polkit設定（YubiKey, U2F認証）
    66→│   │   │   ├── docker.nix
    67→│   │   │   ├── tailscale.nix
    68→│   │   │   ├── android.nix
    69→│   │   │   ├── fonts.nix
    70→│   │   │   └── ssh.nix
    71→│   │   ├── home/                  # home-manager設定
    72→│   │   │   ├── default.nix
    73→│   │   │   ├── packages.nix       # ユーザーパッケージ
    74→│   │   │   ├── niri.nix           # Niri home設定
    75→│   │   │   └── programs/          # プログラム設定
    76→│   │   │       ├── zsh.nix
    77→│   │   │       ├── ghostty/
    78→│   │   │       ├── neovim.nix
    79→│   │   │       ├── tmux.nix
    80→│   │   │       ├── git.nix
    81→│   │   │       ├── vscode.nix
    82→│   │   │       ├── waybar.nix
    83→│   │   │       └── ...
    84→│   │   └── lib/                   # ヘルパー関数
    85→│   │       └── helpers/
    86→│   └── overlays/                  # カスタムパッケージ（overlay形式）
    87→│       ├── default.nix            # 全overlayの集約
    88→│       ├── polycat.nix
    89→│       ├── aqua.nix
    90→│       └── ...
    91→└── nvim/                          # Neovim設定（Lua）
    92→    └── lua/plugins/               # lazy.nvim プラグイン設定
    93→```
    94→
    95→## Key Features
    96→
    97→- **WM**: Niri（スクロール可能なタイリングWM）
    98→- **IME**: fcitx5 + hazkey（LLM変換）
    99→- **YubiKey**: PAM U2F認証サポート（polkit, hyprlock対応）
   100→- **Development**: Docker, Tailscale, Android開発環境
   101→
   102→## Key Aliases
   103→
   104→定義場所: `nix/modules/home/programs/zsh.nix`
   105→
   106→- `rebuild` → `nh os switch`
   107→- `g` → 引数なし: ghq+peco、引数あり: git
   108→- `gh-q` → ghq + fzf でリポジトリ選択・clone
   109→- `yolo` → `claude --dangerously-skip-permissions`
   110→
```

  "content": "# CLAUDE.md\n\nThis file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.\n\n## Commands\n\n```bash\n# NixOS: システム設定を反映（rebuild）\nnh os switch\n# または\nsudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .\n\n# macOS: システム設定を反映（rebuild）\ndarwin-rebuild switch --flake .\n\n# 特定のパッケージを検索\nnix search nixpkgs <package>\n\n# flake入力を更新\nnix flake update\n```\n\n## Agent Skills\n\nこのリポジトリは`agent-skills-nix`でスキルを管理しています。\n\n- **設定**: `nix/modules/home/agent-skills.nix`\n- **ローカルスキル**: `agents/skills/`\n- **外部スキル**: `anthropics/skills`, `vercel-labs/skills`\n- **デプロイ先**: `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.config/claude/skills`\n\n主なスキル：\n- `social-digest` - Discord + Mastodon投稿をObsidianに保存（ローカル）\n- `oura-daily-watch` - Oura Ring データ + Discord行動分析（ローカル）\n- `docx`, `pdf`, `pptx`, `xlsx` - ドキュメント処理（Anthropic）\n- `frontend-design`, `skill-creator`, `webapp-testing` - 開発支援（Anthropic）\n- `find-skills` - スキル検索・発見支援（Vercel）\n- `ui-ux-pro-max` - UI/UXデザインシステム生成（コミュニティ）\n\n詳細: `agents/README.md`\n\n## Architecture\n\nNixOS & macOS flake構成 with home-manager（nixos-unstable）\n\n```\nflake.nix                          # エントリポイント\n├── agents/\n│   └── skills/                    # Claude Codeスキル\n├── raycast/                       # macOS Raycastスクリプト\n├── nix/\n│   ├── hosts/                     # ホスト固有の設定\n│   │   ├── nixos/                 # NixOS (x86_64-linux)\n│   │   │   ├── default.nix        # システム基本設定（boot, network, locale）\n│   │   │   └── hardware-configuration.nix\n│   │   └── darwin/                # macOS (aarch64-darwin)\n│   │       └── default.nix        # ホスト名設定\n│   ├── profiles/                  # プロファイル定義\n│   │   ├── cli-minimal.nix        # 最小CLI環境\n│   │   ├── cli.nix                # CLI環境（docker, tailscale含む）\n│   │   ├── gui.nix                # GUI環境（niri, audio, bluetooth含む）\n│   │   └── darwin.nix             # macOS環境\n│   ├── modules/\n│   │   ├── core/                  # NixOSシステムモジュール\n│   │   │   ├── default.nix\n│   │   │   ├── packages.nix       # システムパッケージ（firefox, zsh, nh, nix-ld）\n│   │   │   ├── user.nix           # ユーザー設定 + home-manager統合\n│   │   │   ├── niri.nix           # Niri WM + greetd\n│   │   │   ├── input.nix          # fcitx5 + hazkey\n│   │   │   ├── audio.nix          # pipewire\n│   │   │   ├── bluetooth.nix\n│   │   │   ├── pam.nix            # PAM/polkit設定（YubiKey, U2F認証）\n│   │   │   ├── docker.nix\n│   │   │   ├── tailscale.nix\n│   │   │   ├── android.nix\n│   │   │   ├── fonts.nix\n│   │   │   └── ssh.nix\n│   │   ├── darwin/                # macOS nix-darwinモジュール\n│   │   │   ├── default.nix\n│   │   │   ├── system.nix         # macOS defaults (Dock, Finder, trackpad等)\n│   │   │   ├── homebrew.nix       # Homebrew cask管理\n│   │   │   ├── fonts.nix          # macOSフォント設定\n│   │   │   └── user.nix           # ユーザー設定 + home-manager統合\n│   │   ├── home/                  # home-manager設定（共通 + プラットフォーム別）\n│   │   │   ├── default.nix        # 共通設定（zsh, git, claude-code等）\n│   │   │   ├── linux.nix          # Linux用エントリポイント\n│   │   │   ├── darwin.nix         # macOS用エントリポイント（1Password Shell Plugins）\n│   │   │   ├── packages.nix       # 共通ユーザーパッケージ\n│   │   │   ├── packages-linux.nix # Linux固有パッケージ\n│   │   │   ├── packages-darwin.nix # macOS固有パッケージ\n│   │   │   ├── niri.nix           # Niri home設定\n│   │   │   └── programs/          # プログラム設定\n│   │   │       ├── zsh.nix\n│   │   │       ├── ghostty/\n│   │   │       ├── neovim.nix\n│   │   │       ├── tmux.nix\n│   │   │       ├── git.nix\n│   │   │       ├── vscode.nix\n│   │   │       ├── waybar.nix\n│   │   │       └── ...\n│   │   └── lib/                   # ヘルパー関数\n│   │       └── helpers/\n│   └── overlays/                  # カスタムパッケージ（overlay形式）\n│       ├── default.nix            # 全overlayの集約\n│       ├── polycat.nix\n│       ├── aqua.nix\n│       └── ...\n└── nvim/                          # Neovim設定（Lua）\n    └── lua/plugins/               # lazy.nvim プラグイン設定\n```\n\n## Key Features\n\n### NixOS\n- **WM**: Niri（スクロール可能なタイリングWM）\n- **IME**: fcitx5 + hazkey（LLM変換）\n- **YubiKey**: PAM U2F認証サポート（polkit, hyprlock対応）\n- **Development**: Docker, Tailscale, Android開発環境\n\n### macOS\n- **Homebrew**: GUI アプリ管理（Ghostty, Raycast, Chrome等）\n- **Touch ID**: sudo認証対応\n- **1Password**: Shell Plugins（gh, awscli2）\n\n## Key Aliases\n\n定義場所: `zsh/config/aliases.zsh`\n\n- `rebuild` → NixOS: `nh os switch` / macOS: `darwin-rebuild switch`\n- `g` → 引数なし: ghq+peco、引数あり: git\n- `gh-q` → ghq + fzf でリポジトリ選択・clone\n- `yolo` → `claude --dangerously-skip-permissions`\n"
}

    88→      # Local agent skills (filtered from this repo)
    89→      local-skills = nix-filter.lib {
    90→        root = self;
    91→        include = [ "agents/skills" ];
    92→      };
    93→
    94→      # Overlay for external flake packages
    95→      externalOverlay = final: prev: {
    96→        claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.claude-code;
    97→        ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.ccusage;
    98→        codex = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.codex;
    99→        opencode = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.opencode;
   100→        vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.vibe-kanban;
   101→        version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${final.system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
   102→          doCheck = false;
   103→        });
   104→        gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${final.system}.default;
   105→      } // (if final.stdenv.isLinux then {
   106→        ghostty = ghostty.packages.${final.system}.default;
   107→      } else {});
   108→
   109→      # Custom package overlays
   110→      customOverlay = import ./nix/overlays;
   111→
   112→      # mkSystem helper function (NixOS)
   113→      mkSystem =
   114→        {
   115→          host,
   116→          system,
   117→          profile,
   118→          extraModules ? [ ],
   119→        }:
   120→        let
   121→          dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/home/yuta";
   122→        in
   123→        nixpkgs.lib.nixosSystem {
   124→          inherit system;
   125→          specialArgs = {
   126→            inherit helpers dotfilesDir home-manager niri local-skills anthropic-skills vercel-skills ui-ux-pro-max-skill agent-skills;
   127→          };
   128→          modules = [
   129→            ./nix/modules/core
   130→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   131→            ./nix/profiles/${profile}.nix
   132→            {
   133→              nixpkgs.overlays = [ externalOverlay moonbit-overlay.overlays.default customOverlay ];
   134→              nixpkgs.config.allowUnfreePredicate =
   135→                pkg: builtins.elem (nixpkgs.lib.getName pkg) [
   136→                  "claude-code"
   137→                  "android-studio"
   138→                  "vscode"
   139→                  "google-chrome"
   140→                  "discord"
   141→                  "slack"
   142→                  "obsidian"
   143→                  "1password"
   144→                  "insomnia"
   145→                  "spotify"
   146→                ];
   147→            }
   148→            nix-hazkey.nixosModules.hazkey
   149→          ] ++ extraModules;
   150→        };
   151→
   152→      # mkDarwin helper function (macOS)
   153→      mkDarwin =
   154→        {
   155→          host,
   156→          system,
   157→          extraModules ? [ ],
   158→        }:
   159→        let
   160→          dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/Users/yuta";
   161→        in
   162→        nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
   163→          inherit system;
   164→          specialArgs = {
   165→            inherit helpers dotfilesDir home-manager local-skills anthropic-skills vercel-skills ui-ux-pro-max-skill agent-skills _1password-shell-plugins;
   166→          };
   167→          modules = [
   168→            ./nix/modules/darwin
   169→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   170→            ./nix/profiles/darwin.nix
   171→            {
   172→              nixpkgs.overlays = [ externalOverlay moonbit-overlay.overlays.default customOverlay ];
   173→              nixpkgs.config.allowUnfreePredicate =
   174→                pkg: builtins.elem (nixpkgs.lib.getName pkg) [
   175→                  "claude-code"
   176→                  "vscode"
   177→                  "discord"
   178→                  "slack"
   179→                  "obsidian"
   180→                  "1password"
   181→                  "spotify"
   182→                ];
   183→            }
   184→          ] ++ extraModules;
   185→        };
   186→    in
   187→    {
   188→      nixosConfigurations = {
   189→        nixos = mkSystem {
   190→          host = "nixos";
   191→          system = "x86_64-linux";
   192→          profile = "gui";
   193→          extraModules = [ ];
   194→        };
   195→      };
   196→
   197→      darwinConfigurations = {
   198→        darwin = mkDarwin {
   199→          host = "darwin";
   200→          system = "aarch64-darwin";
   201→        };
   202→      };
   203→    };
   204→}
   205→

`final.stdenv.isLinux` は overlay 内で無限再帰を引き起こします。`final.system` を使ってシステム文字列で判定に変更します。

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01SeGcg4qniP4FBmfCUC5UtG
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix",
  "old_string": "      } // (if final.stdenv.isLinux then {\n        ghostty = ghostty.packages.${final.system}.default;\n      } else {});",
  "new_string": "      } // (if builtins.match \".*-linux\" final.system != null then {\n        ghostty = ghostty.packages.${final.system}.default;\n      } else {});"
}

> AGENT

`final.system` も overlay 内で再帰を引き起こしています。overlay を system パラメータ付き関数に変更します。

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01NYS2zSZYuZcBtp5uzNu8Eg
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/flake.nix",
  "old_string": "      # Overlay for external flake packages\n      externalOverlay = final: prev: {\n        claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.claude-code;\n        ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.ccusage;\n        codex = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.codex;\n        opencode = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.opencode;\n        vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${final.system}.vibe-kanban;\n        version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${final.system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {\n          doCheck = false;\n        });\n        gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${final.system}.default;\n      } // (if builtins.match \".*-linux\" final.system != null then {\n        ghostty = ghostty.packages.${final.system}.default;\n      } else {});",
  "new_string": "      # Overlay for external flake packages (parameterized by system)\n      mkExternalOverlay = system: final: prev: {\n        claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${system}.claude-code;\n        ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${system}.ccusage;\n        codex = llm-agents.packages.${system}.codex;\n        opencode = llm-agents.packages.${system}.opencode;\n        vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${system}.vibe-kanban;\n        version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {\n          doCheck = false;\n        });\n        gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${system}.default;\n      } // (if builtins.match \".*-linux\" system != null then {\n        ghostty = ghostty.packages.${system}.default;\n      } else {});"
}

> AGENT

全ての実装が完了し、`nix flake check` が通りました。変更内容のサマリー:

### 新規ファイル (14ファイル)
- `nix/modules/darwin/` - macOS nix-darwin モジュール一式 (default, system, homebrew, fonts, user)
- `nix/modules/home/linux.nix` - NixOS 用 home-manager エントリポイント
- `nix/modules/home/darwin.nix` - macOS 用 home-manager エントリポイント (1Password Shell Plugins 統合)
- `nix/modules/home/packages-linux.nix` - Linux 固有パッケージ
- `nix/modules/home/packages-darwin.nix` - macOS 固有パッケージ
- `nix/hosts/darwin/default.nix` - Darwin ホスト設定
- `nix/profiles/darwin.nix` - macOS プロファイル
- `raycast/` - Raycast スクリプト (learn-nix から移植)

### 変更ファイル (10ファイル)
- `flake.nix` - nix-darwin/1password inputs 追加、`mkExternalOverlay` (system パラメータ化)、`mkDarwin` 関数、`darwinConfigurations` 追加
- `nix/modules/home/default.nix` - `homeDirectory` ハードコード削除
- `nix/modules/home/packages.nix` - 共通パッケージのみに分離
- `nix/modules/core/user.nix` - import パスを `linux.nix` に変更
- `nix/profiles/cli.nix` / `cli-server.nix` - `packages-linux.nix` の import 追加
- `nix/overlays/keifu.nix` - Darwin の apple_sdk frameworks 追加
- `zsh/config/aliases.zsh` - `uname` ベースのプラットフォーム分岐
- `AGENTS.md` - Darwin コマンド・アーキテクチャ追記

### 検証結果

tool_result
id: toolu_018E1dLt1DEdqFbsqotgorue
```
{"result":"The `ryoppippi/dotfiles` repository manages its `Brewfile` by symlinking a separate `Brewfile` into the user's home directory, rather than declaratively managing it solely through `nix-darwin`'s Homebrew module. This symlinking mechanism is handled by a Home Manager activation script. \n\n### Brewfile Management Mechanism\n\nThe `Brewfile` is managed through a Home Manager activation script defined in `nix/modules/darwin/dotfiles.nix`.  Specifically, the `home.activation.linkDotfilesDarwin` entry, which runs after `linkGeneration`,  includes a command to symlink the `Brewfile`. \n\nThe relevant code snippet is:\n```nix\n      # Homebrew bundle file\n      link_force \"${dotfilesDir}/Brewfile\" \"${homeDirectory}/.Brewfile\"\n```\nThis command uses the `link_force` helper  to create a symbolic link from the `Brewfile` located within the `dotfilesDir` (which defaults to `~/ghq/github.com/ryoppippi/dotfiles`)  to `~/.Brewfile`. \n\n### Homebrew Module Integration\n\nWhile the `Brewfile` itself is symlinked, `nix-darwin`'s Homebrew module is also extensively used for managing Homebrew taps, brews, casks, and Mac App Store applications.  This declarative management is configured in `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` under the `homebrew` option.  For example, it enables Homebrew, sets cleanup behavior, defines taps like `arto-app/tap`,  installs brews such as `mas`,  and lists numerous casks and `masApps`. \n\nTherefore, the system uses a hybrid approach: a symlinked `Brewfile` for certain Homebrew bundle functionalities, and `nix-darwin`'s declarative Homebrew module for direct management of taps, brews, casks, and `masApps`.  \n\n## Notes\n\nThe `README.md` also mentions that \"Homebrew packages (taps, brews, casks, masApps)\" are managed by `nix/darwin.nix` (which corresponds to `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix`),  and that \"Dotfiles symlinks using `home.file`\" are part of `nix/home.nix` (which corresponds to `nix/modules/home/dotfiles.nix` and other home modules).  The `Platform-Specific Configuration` wiki page further clarifies that `nix/modules/darwin/dotfiles.nix` handles the `Brewfile` symlink.  This confirms the dual approach to Homebrew management.\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Platform-Specific Configuration (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#2.2)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

> AGENT

ryoppippi/dotfiles ではハイブリッド方式を採用しています:

1. **nix-darwin の `homebrew` モジュール** (`nix/modules/darwin/system.nix`): taps, brews, casks, masApps を宣言的に管理
2. **Brewfile の symlink** (`nix/modules/darwin/dotfiles.nix`): リポジトリ内の `Brewfile` を `~/.Brewfile` にリンク

```nix
# darwin/dotfiles.nix の activation script 内
link_force "${dotfilesDir}/Brewfile" "${homeDirectory}/.Brewfile"
```

つまり、nix-darwin の `homebrew.casks = [ ... ]` で宣言的に管理しつつ、別途 `Brewfile` も symlink して `brew bundle` でも使えるようにしている二重管理の形です。

現在の dotnix では `nix/modules/darwin/homebrew.nix` で casks を宣言的に管理しているので、`brew bundle` も使いたい場合は Brewfile を追加して symlink する形になります。必要であれば追加しましょうか？

```
{"result":"Ryoppippi uses both `nix-darwin`'s Homebrew module and a separate symlinked `Brewfile` to manage Homebrew packages on macOS  . The `nix-darwin` Homebrew module handles taps, brews, casks, and Mac App Store applications directly within the Nix configuration , while the symlinked `Brewfile` is intended for use with `brew bundle` for additional packages not managed by Nix . The `Brewfile` is not directly referenced by `nix-darwin` for package installation .\n\n## Homebrew Configuration in `nix-darwin`\n\nThe `nix-darwin` configuration in `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` explicitly enables Homebrew integration . It defines:\n*   **Taps**: `arto-app/tap` .\n*   **Brews**: `mas` .\n*   **Casks**: A list of 18 casks including `1password`, `alfred`, `arc`, `discord`, and `karabiner-elements` . Notably, `imageoptim` is included here because `brew-nix` (used for other casks) had issues with its `.tar.xz` extraction .\n*   **Mac App Store Apps (`masApps`)**: A comprehensive list of 39 applications with their respective App Store IDs, such as `Xcode`, `Slack`, and `Microsoft Excel` .\n\nThis configuration is applied when the `nix-darwin` system is switched .\n\n## The Symlinked `Brewfile`\n\nA separate `Brewfile` is symlinked to `~/.Brewfile` via a Home Manager activation script defined in `nix/modules/darwin/dotfiles.nix` . This symlink is created during the Home Manager activation process .\n\nThe `bash/.bashrc` file explicitly sets the `HOMEBREW_BUNDLE_FILE` environment variable to `~/.Brewfile` , indicating that this `Brewfile` is intended to be used with `brew bundle`.\n\n## Comparison and Purpose\n\nThe `Brewfile` contains additional Homebrew packages that are not managed directly by the `nix-darwin` Homebrew module. The `nix-darwin` module focuses on system-level Homebrew installations and Mac App Store apps . The `Brewfile` likely includes user-specific or less critical applications that the user prefers to manage with `brew bundle` separately, outside of the declarative Nix system.\n\nThe actual contents of the `Brewfile` are not provided in the given snippets, so a direct comparison of its contents with `system.nix` is not possible. However, based on the configuration, the `Brewfile` would contain `brew` and `cask` entries that are not present in the `homebrew` section of `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `brew-nix` overlay is used to manage some Homebrew casks via Nix, as seen in `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` . This is distinct from the `homebrew` module in `system.nix` . The `brew-nix` packages are added to `home.packages` , while the `system.nix` Homebrew section manages system-wide Homebrew installations. The `Brewfile` provides a third mechanism for Homebrew package management, likely for packages not suitable for either the `nix-darwin` module or `brew-nix`.\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Platform-Specific Configuration (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#2.2)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

> AGENT

ryoppippi は実際には**3層構造**で Homebrew を管理しています:

1. **nix-darwin `homebrew` モジュール** (`system.nix`) — `darwin-rebuild switch` 時に自動適用。casks, masApps 等
2. **brew-nix overlay** (`packages.nix`) — Homebrew cask を Nix パッケージとして `home.packages` に入れる（hash 管理が必要）
3. **Brewfile symlink** (`dotfiles.nix`) — `brew bundle` 用。`HOMEBREW_BUNDLE_FILE=~/.Brewfile` を設定

Brewfile が別途存在する理由は、nix-darwin の `homebrew` モジュールや brew-nix では扱いにくいパッケージ（特定のオプション付き brew 等）を `brew bundle` で別管理するためです。

ただし、DeepWiki からは Brewfile の中身が確認できなかったので、実際にどこまで分離しているかは不明です。ryoppippi のケースではかなり凝った構成ですが、dotnix では **nix-darwin の `homebrew` モジュール1本で管理する今の方式で十分**だと思います。`brew bundle` が別途必要になったら Brewfile を追加すれば良いだけなので。

{
  "repoName": "ryoppippi/dotfiles",
  "question": "Why does ryoppippi use brew-nix overlay in addition to nix-darwin's homebrew module? What is the advantage of using brew-nix to install Homebrew casks as Nix packages via home.packages instead of just using the nix-darwin homebrew module? Show the brew-nix overlay setup in flake.nix and the packages managed via brew-nix in packages.nix. What problem does brew-nix solve that nix-darwin's homebrew module doesn't?"
}

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_0186F2LHoCN6xPC2GkKNTKVx
```
{"result":"ryoppippi uses the `brew-nix` overlay in addition to `nix-darwin`'s Homebrew module to manage Homebrew casks as Nix packages, allowing them to be installed via `home.packages` . This approach integrates Homebrew cask management more deeply into the Nix declarative configuration, providing benefits like version pinning and reproducibility for these applications . The primary advantage of `brew-nix` is its ability to treat Homebrew casks as Nix packages, which `nix-darwin`'s native Homebrew module does not offer for casks .\n\n## brew-nix Overlay Setup\n\nThe `brew-nix` overlay is set up in `flake.nix` . It is included as an input to the flake  and then conditionally applied as an overlay to the `pkgs` set for Darwin systems within the `mkPkgs` function .\n\nSpecifically, the `brew-nix` input is defined as:\n```nix\n    brew-nix = {\n      url = \"github:BatteredBunny/brew-nix\";\n      inputs = {\n        brew-api.follows = \"brew-api\";\n        nix-darwin.follows = \"nix-darwin\";\n        nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n      };\n    };\n``` \nThis input ensures that `brew-nix` uses the same `brew-api`, `nix-darwin`, and `nixpkgs` versions as the rest of the flake, promoting consistency .\n\nThe `brew-nix.overlays.default` is then added to the `overlays` list within the `mkPkgs` function, but only if the system is Darwin . This makes the packages provided by `brew-nix` available in the `pkgs` set.\n\n## Packages Managed via brew-nix\n\nHomebrew casks installed via `brew-nix` are listed in `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` . These packages are accessed through the `pkgs.brewCasks` namespace .\n\nExamples of such packages include `alt-tab`, `appcleaner`, `raycast`, and `zed` . Some `brew-nix` packages, like `quitter` and `suspicious-package`, require `hash` overrides due to their `src` definitions .\n\n## Problem Solved by brew-nix\n\nThe `nix-darwin` Homebrew module primarily manages traditional Homebrew installations, including taps, brews (command-line tools), and casks that typically install to `/Applications` . However, it does not treat Homebrew casks as Nix packages that can be directly included in `home.packages` .\n\n`brew-nix` solves this by providing an overlay that converts Homebrew cask definitions into Nix derivations . This allows these GUI applications to be managed declaratively alongside other Nix packages within `home.packages` . This integration means that `brew-nix` packages benefit from Nix's reproducibility, atomic upgrades, and rollbacks, which are not inherently available for casks installed directly via `nix-darwin`'s Homebrew module .\n\nFor instance, `imageoptim` is explicitly excluded from `brew-nix` usage due to `tar.xz` extraction issues, indicating that `brew-nix` is specifically chosen for casks that can be successfully packaged as Nix derivations . Other casks, such as `karabiner-elements` and `1password`, are still managed directly by `nix-darwin`'s Homebrew module because they require installation to `/Applications` .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `Overview` wiki page provides a good summary of the `brew-nix` integration, noting that it enables fetching Homebrew casks as Nix derivations from `brew-api` . It also clarifies the distinction between `brew-nix` managed casks and those still handled by the traditional `nix-darwin` Homebrew module .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Overview (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#1)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

> AGENT

brew-nix を使う理由は **Homebrew cask を Nix パッケージとして扱える**ことです。

### nix-darwin の `homebrew` モジュール
- `brew install --cask` を裏で実行するだけ
- `/Applications` にインストールされる
- Nix の管理外（ロールバック不可、バージョン固定不可）
- Homebrew 自体が事前にインストールされている必要がある

### brew-nix
- Homebrew cask の定義を **Nix derivation に変換**する overlay
- `home.packages` に入れて Nix と同じように宣言的に管理できる
- バージョン固定、アトミックな更新、ロールバックが効く
- `pkgs.brewCasks.raycast` のように参照

### なぜ両方使うのか

全ての cask が brew-nix で動くわけではないからです。例えば:
- `karabiner-elements`, `1password` → `/Applications` への直接インストールが必要 → nix-darwin の homebrew モジュール
- `raycast`, `alt-tab`, `appcleaner` → brew-nix で Nix パッケージ化可能 → `home.packages`
- `imageoptim` → tar.xz の展開に問題がある → nix-darwin の homebrew モジュール

要するに「Nix の再現性を最大限活用したいが、全 cask が対応できるわけではない」ので使い分けているという話です。dotnix では現状 nix-darwin の homebrew モジュール一本で十分ですが、再現性を重視するなら brew-nix の導入も検討する価値はあります。

The mkPkgs function showing how brew-nix overlay is conditionally applied for Darwin\n3. The complete contents of nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix showing all brewCasks packages and hash overrides\n4. How brewCasks packages are referenced in home.packages"

```
{"result":"The `brew-nix` setup in this repository involves defining `brew-nix` and `brew-api` as flake inputs, conditionally applying the `brew-nix` overlay for Darwin systems within the `mkPkgs` function, and listing `brewCasks` packages with hash overrides in `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` for inclusion in `home.packages`.\n\n### 1. `flake.nix` inputs for `brew-nix` and `brew-api`\n\nThe `flake.nix` file defines `brew-nix` and `brew-api` as inputs .\nThe `brew-nix` input specifies its URL as `github:BatteredBunny/brew-nix` and declares that it follows `brew-api`, `nix-darwin`, and `nixpkgs` inputs .\nThe `brew-api` input specifies its URL as `github:BatteredBunny/brew-api` and sets `flake = false` . The `flake.lock` file confirms these inputs and their locked versions .\n\n### 2. `mkPkgs` function and conditional `brew-nix` overlay for Darwin\n\nThe `mkPkgs` function is defined in `flake.nix` . This function takes a `system` argument and determines if the system is Darwin by checking if it matches `.*-darwin` .\nThe `brew-nix.overlays.default` overlay is conditionally applied to the `nixpkgs` import only if `isDarwin` is true . This ensures that `brew-nix` packages are only available on macOS systems. The `mkPkgs` function is used to create the `pkgs` set for both Darwin and Linux configurations   .\n\n### 3. Contents of `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix`\n\nThe `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` file defines macOS-specific packages for `home.packages` .\nIt includes a list of standard macOS packages .\nAdditionally, it uses `pkgs.brewCasks` to include a list of Homebrew casks managed via Nix .\nTwo `brewCasks` packages, `quitter` and `suspicious-package`, require hash overrides due to potential issues with `brew-nix`'s default fetching mechanism . These overrides explicitly define the `src` and `hash` for these packages  .\n\n### 4. How `brewCasks` packages are referenced in `home.packages`\n\nThe `brewCasks` packages are referenced directly within the `home.packages` list in `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` . The `with pkgs.brewCasks;` construct allows direct access to the packages provided by the `brew-nix` overlay . For packages requiring hash overrides, they are explicitly defined as overridden attributes of `pkgs.brewCasks` and appended to the `home.packages` list . This `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` file is then imported into the `home-manager` configuration for Darwin systems .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `brew-nix` integration is specifically for macOS (Darwin) systems, as indicated by the conditional overlay application in `mkPkgs` . Traditional Homebrew is still used for certain casks that require installation into `/Applications`, as seen in `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` . The `imageoptim` cask is explicitly excluded from `brew-nix` due to `tar.xz` extraction issues .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Overview (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#1)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.com/search/show-me-the-complete-brewnix-s_a4cfd095-a8a5-4d41-bc6a-04aecef55330\n"}
```

{"result":"You're asking for specific Nix code snippets related to `brew-nix`, `brew-api`, and the `mkPkgs` function, as well as the full content of `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix`   .\n\n### 1. `brew-nix` and `brew-api` input declarations in `flake.nix`\n\nThe `brew-nix` input declaration specifies its URL and defines that its `brew-api`, `nix-darwin`, and `nixpkgs` inputs should follow the respective inputs of the main `flake.nix` . The `brew-api` input declaration specifies its URL and sets `flake = false` because it's not a Nix flake itself .\n\n```nix\n    brew-nix = {\n      url = \"github:BatteredBunny/brew-nix\";\n      inputs = {\n        brew-api.follows = \"brew-api\";\n        nix-darwin.follows = \"nix-darwin\";\n        nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n      };\n    };\n\n    brew-api = {\n      url = \"github:BatteredBunny/brew-api\";\n      flake = false;\n    };\n``` \n\n### 2. `mkPkgs` function implementation\n\nThe `mkPkgs` function takes a `system` as an argument . It determines if the system is Darwin by checking if the `system` string matches `.*-darwin` . It then imports `nixpkgs` with `allowUnfree = true` and applies a list of overlays . The `brew-nix.overlays.default` overlay is conditionally applied only if `isDarwin` is true .\n\n```nix\n      mkPkgs =\n        system:\n        let\n          isDarwin = builtins.match \".*-darwin\" system != null;\n        in\n        import nixpkgs {\n          inherit system;\n          config.allowUnfree = true;\n          overlays = [\n            (_final: _prev: {\n              _llm-agents = llm-agents;\n              _claude-code-overlay = claude-code-overlay;\n            })\n            gh-nippou.overlays.default\n            gh-graph.overlays.default\n            (import ./nix/overlays/default.nix)\n          ]\n          ++ nixpkgs.lib.optionals isDarwin [\n            brew-nix.overlays.default\n          ];\n        };\n``` \n\n### 3. Complete `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` file contents\n\nThis file defines macOS-specific packages and Homebrew casks managed via Nix . It includes a list of `brewCasks` and explicitly overrides the `src` and `hash` for `quitter` and `suspicious-package` due to potential issues .\n\n```nix\n{ pkgs, ... }:\n{\n  home.packages =\n    with pkgs;\n    [\n      # macOS-specific packages\n      ghostty-bin\n      chafa\n      blueutil\n      bluetooth-connector\n      switchaudio-osx\n      terminal-notifier\n      mas\n      audio-priority-bar\n\n      # macOS GUI applications (not available on Linux in nixpkgs)\n      cyberduck\n      keycastr\n      monitorcontrol\n      obsidian\n    ]\n    # brew-nix packages (Homebrew casks managed via Nix)\n    # Note: imageoptim excluded due to tar.xz extraction issues with brew-nix\n    ++ (with pkgs.brewCasks; [\n      alt-tab\n      appcleaner\n      beekeeper-studio\n      bluesnooze\n      chatgpt\n      cursor\n      deskpad\n      figma\n      glance-chamburr\n      homerow\n      istherenet\n      kap\n      maestral\n      marta\n      obs\n      postman\n      processing\n      qlvideo\n      raycast\n      shottr\n      signal\n      stats\n      vlc\n      yaak\n      zed\n      zoom\n    ])\n    # brew-nix packages requiring hash override\n    ++ [\n      (pkgs.brewCasks.quitter.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {\n        src = pkgs.fetchurl {\n          url = builtins.head oldAttrs.src.urls;\n          hash = \"REDACTED\";\n        };\n      }))\n      (pkgs.brewCasks.suspicious-package.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {\n        src = pkgs.fetchurl {\n          url = builtins.head oldAttrs.src.urls;\n          hash = \"REDACTED\";\n        };\n      }))\n    ];\n}\n``` \n\n## Notes\n\nThe `mkPkgs` function is crucial for how packages are made available throughout the flake, especially for macOS, where `brew-nix` overlays are conditionally applied . The `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` file demonstrates how `brew-nix` is used to manage Homebrew casks declaratively within the Nix environment, including handling specific hash overrides for certain packages .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Overview (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#1)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.com/search/show-me-the-exact-code-of-1-th_e260352b-404c-48be-bd62-3ee77bf5e4f0\n"}

> AGENT

コミット完了。変更内容:

- **flake.nix**: `brew-nix` + `brew-api` inputs 追加、Darwin overlays に `brew-nix.overlays.default` 追加
- **packages-darwin.nix**: `pkgs.brewCasks` 経由で raycast, discord, slack, spotify, obsidian を管理
- **homebrew.nix**: brew-nix に移行した cask を除外、`/Applications` 必須の ghostty, chrome, 1password, android-studio のみ残す

brew-nix 管理のパッケージは Nix のバージョン固定・ロールバックが効きます。`/Applications` への直接インストールが必要な cask（1password, ghostty 等）は従来通り homebrew モジュールで管理しています。

> AGENT

コミット完了。整理結果のサマリー:

### 振り分けの考え方

| 管理方法 | 基準 | 件数 |
|---------|------|------|
| **共通 packages.nix** | Linux/macOS 両方で使う CLI ツール | +10 (git-lfs, cmake, ffmpeg, typst, mise 等) |
| **brew-nix** (packages-darwin.nix) | 標準的な .app バンドルの GUI アプリ | ~60 casks |
| **homebrew.nix** | システム統合・カーネル拡張・複雑インストーラー | ~30 casks |
| **fonts.nix** | Homebrew の font-* cask → Nix フォント | +2 (hack nerd font, M+ font) |
| **管理不要** | formulae のライブラリ依存 (libpng, openssl 等) | ~150 (Nix が自動解決) |

### homebrew.nix に残した理由
- **1password, karabiner-elements, orbstack**: システムレベルの権限・拡張が必要
- **ghostty, google-chrome**: `/Applications` への直接インストールが必要
- **adobe-creative-cloud**: 独自インストーラー
- **tailscale, cloudflare-warp**: システム VPN サービス
- **blackhole-2ch**: オーディオドライバ

実際に Mac で `darwin-rebuild switch` したとき、brew-nix で一部の cask がうまく動かないケースがあれば、そのまま homebrew.nix に移すだけで対応できます。

> AGENT

Mac で以下を実行してください:

```bash
echo "=== Homebrew cask: インストール済みだがアプリが存在しない ===" && \
for cask in $(brew list --cask); do
  app_path=$(brew info --cask "$cask" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '/Applications/.*\.app' | head -1)
  if [ -n "$app_path" ] && [ ! -d "$app_path" ]; then
    echo "  $cask → $app_path (missing)"
  fi
done

echo ""
echo "=== /Applications: Homebrew/Nix管理外のアプリ ===" && \
brew_apps=$(brew list --cask --json=v2 2>/dev/null | python3 -c "
import json,sys
data=json.load(sys.stdin)
for c in data.get('casks',[]):
    for a in c.get('artifacts',[]):
        if isinstance(a,dict) and 'app' in a:
            for app in a['app']:
                print(app.split('/')[-1].replace('.app',''))
" 2>/dev/null)
for app in /Applications/*.app; do
  name=$(basename "$app" .app)
  if ! echo "$brew_apps" | grep -qi "$name"; then
    echo "  $name"
  fi
done
```

1つ目は「brew では入ってるのに .app が消されてるもの」、2つ目は「/Applications にあるけど brew 管理外のもの」を出します。

> DEVELOPER

めっちゃ遅いんだけどなぜ

> AGENT

`brew info --cask` を1つずつ呼んでるからです。一括取得に変えます:

```bash
echo "=== Homebrew cask: アプリが存在しない ===" && \
brew list --cask --json=v2 2>/dev/null | python3 -c "
import json,sys,os
data=json.load(sys.stdin)
for c in data.get('casks',[]):
    for a in c.get('artifacts',[]):
        if isinstance(a,dict) and 'app' in a:
            for app in a['app']:
                path='/Applications/'+app if not app.startswith('/') else app
                if not os.path.isdir(path):
                    print(f\"  {c['token']} → {path} (missing)\")
"

echo ""
echo "=== /Applications: Homebrew管理外のアプリ ===" && \
brew list --cask --json=v2 2>/dev/null | python3 -c "
import json,sys,os,glob
data=json.load(sys.stdin)
managed=set()
for c in data.get('casks',[]):
    for a in c.get('artifacts',[]):
        if isinstance(a,dict) and 'app' in a:
            for app in a['app']:
                managed.add(app.split('/')[-1].replace('.app','').lower())
for app in sorted(glob.glob('/Applications/*.app')):
    name=os.path.basename(app).replace('.app','')
    if name.lower() not in managed:
        print(f'  {name}')
"
```

`brew list --cask --json=v2` で全 cask 情報を1回の呼び出しで取得するので、数秒で終わるはずです。

> AGENT

`--json=v2` は `brew info` のオプションでした。`brew list` では使えません。修正版:

```bash
brew info --cask --json=v2 $(brew list --cask) | python3 -c "import json,sys,os;data=json.load(sys.stdin);casks=data.get('casks',data) if isinstance(data,dict) else data;[print(c['token']) for c in casks for a in c.get('artifacts',[]) if isinstance(a,dict) and 'app' in a for app in a['app'] if not os.path.isdir('/Applications/'+app)]"
```

```bash
brew info --cask --json=v2 $(brew list --cask) | python3 -c "import json,sys,os,glob;data=json.load(sys.stdin);casks=data.get('casks',data) if isinstance(data,dict) else data;managed={app.split('/')[-1].replace('.app','').lower() for c in casks for a in c.get('artifacts',[]) if isinstance(a,dict) and 'app' in a for app in a['app']};[print(os.path.basename(a).replace('.app','')) for a in sorted(glob.glob('/Applications/*.app')) if os.path.basename(a).replace('.app','').lower() not in managed]"
```

> AGENT

2つ問題があります。シェルが長い行を折り返して Python の構文エラーになっていることと、`font-mplus` が存在しないこと。スクリプトファイルにします:

```bash
cat > /tmp/check_apps.py << 'EOF'
import json, sys, os, glob

data = json.load(sys.stdin)
casks = data.get("casks", data) if isinstance(data, dict) else data

managed = set()
missing_app = []

for c in casks:
    for a in c.get("artifacts", []):
        if isinstance(a, dict) and "app" in a:
            for app in a["app"]:
                managed.add(app.split("/")[-1].replace(".app", "").lower())
                path = "/Applications/" + app
                if not os.path.isdir(path):
                    missing_app.append(c["token"])

print("=== brew cask: .appが見つからない ===")
for t in missing_app:
    print(f"  {t}")

print()
print("=== /Applications: brew管理外 ===")
for app in sorted(glob.glob("/Applications/*.app")):
    name = os.path.basename(app).replace(".app", "")
    if name.lower() not in managed:
        print(f"  {name}")
EOF

brew info --cask --json=v2 $(brew list --cask 2>/dev/null | grep -v '^font-') 2>/dev/null | python3 /tmp/check_apps.py
```

> AGENT

そうですね。純粋にシェルでやります:

```bash
echo "=== brew cask: .appが見つからない ===" && brew list --cask | grep -v '^font-' | while read c; do app=$(brew info --cask "$c" 2>/dev/null | grep "^/Applications/" | head -1); [ -n "$app" ] && [ ! -d "$app" ] && echo "  $c"; done
```

```bash
echo "=== /Applications: brew管理外 ===" && diff <(brew list --cask | grep -v '^font-' | while read c; do brew info --cask "$c" 2>/dev/null | grep -o '/Applications/[^(]*\.app' | head -1; done | xargs -I{} basename {} .app | sort -f) <(ls /Applications/ | sed 's/\.app$//' | sort -f) | grep '^>' | sed 's/^> /  /'
```

ただし `brew info` を1つずつ呼ぶので遅いです。速くするなら `brew` の JSON は避けられないので、jq を使う方法:

```bash
brew list --cask | grep -v '^font-' | xargs brew info --cask --json=v2 | jq -r '.casks[] | .token as $t | .artifacts[] | objects | .app? // empty | .[] | "\($t)\t\(.)"' > /tmp/brew_cask_apps.tsv
```

```bash
echo "=== brew cask: .appが見つからない ===" && while IFS=$'\t' read t app; do [ ! -d "/Applications/$app" ] && echo "  $t"; done < /tmp/brew_cask_apps.tsv
```

```bash
echo "=== /Applications: brew管理外 ===" && comm -23 <(ls /Applications/ | sed 's/\.app$//' | sort -f) <(cut -f2 /tmp/brew_cask_apps.tsv | xargs -I{} basename {} .app | sort -f) | sed 's/^/  /'
```

jq 版は `brew info` を1回で済ませるので速いです。Mac に jq が入っていれば下の3コマンドを順に実行してください。

> AGENT

結果を整理します。

### 1. brew に残ってるけど .app がないもの（`brew uninstall --cask` で掃除可能）

実際にもう使ってないなら、Mac で以下を実行して一括削除できます:

```bash
brew uninstall --cask kiro-cli aqua-voice automattic-texts blockbench bruno cleanmymac collie deepl diffusionbee dropbox fig figma grammarly hyper jquake krita lagrange linear-linear local mem mimestream minecraft minecraft-education netron notchnook nvidia-geforce-now obs ollama-app osu playcover-community postman proxyman qgis rstudio sidequest superwhisper tableplus vlc warp webstorm wechat zed
```

まだ使いたいものがあれば、上のリストから外してください。

### 2. /Applications にあるけど dotnix で未管理のもの

| アプリ | 対応 |
|--------|------|
| Adobe Lightroom CC / Classic | homebrew.nix に追加 |
| Cubase 14 / Groove Agent SE / HALion Sonic / Steinberg 関連 / VOCALOID | Steinberg 独自インストーラー → homebrew.nix に入れられない。手動管理のまま |
| eTax / MynaPortalApp | 政府系アプリ → 手動管理のまま |
| Google Chrome Canary | brew-nix に追加可能 |
| Screen Studio | brew-nix に追加可能 |
| Telegram | brew-nix に追加可能 |
| Yubico Authenticator | brew-nix に追加可能 |
| Developer (Xcode) | App Store / xcode-select → masApps で管理可能 |
| Safari / Utilities / Nix Apps | システム/Nix → 管理不要 |

### 3. 名前のズレで「管理外」に見えてるけど実は管理済み

1Password, ChatGPT, Ghostty, Google Chrome, Halloy, Keybase, Mattermost, Messenger, Notion Calendar, Obsidian, OrbStack, Raycast, Signal, SimpleX, Slack, Spotify, Tailscale, zoom.us — これらは既に homebrew.nix または packages-darwin.nix で管理済みです。

dotnix に追加すべきものを反映しましょうか？（Lightroom, Chrome Canary, Screen Studio, Telegram, Yubico Authenticator）

| **wechat** | WeChat メッセンジャー | |

> AGENT

先ほどの2つ目のコマンドの結果から、brew 管理外で `/Applications` にあったアプリのうち、**dotnix でも未管理のもの**を抽出します:

| アプリ | 種類 | 対応案 |
|--------|------|--------|
| Adobe Lightroom CC | 写真編集 | homebrew.nix (`adobe-lightroom`) |
| Adobe Lightroom Classic | 写真編集 | homebrew.nix (`adobe-lightroom-classic`) |
| Cubase 14 | DAW (Steinberg) | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| eTax | 確定申告 (国税庁) | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| Google Chrome Canary | Chrome 開発版 | brew-nix (`google-chrome-canary`) |
| Groove Agent SE | ドラム音源 (Steinberg) | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| HALion Sonic | シンセ音源 (Steinberg) | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| License Control Center | Steinberg ライセンス管理 | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| MynaPortalApp | マイナポータル | App Store → masApps |
| Nani | ? | 不明 |
| Recogra | ? | 不明 |
| Screen Studio | 画面録画 (有料) | brew-nix (`screen-studio`) |
| Steinberg Activation Manager | Steinberg 認証 | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| Steinberg Download Assistant | Steinberg DL | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| Steinberg Library Manager | Steinberg ライブラリ | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| Telegram | メッセンジャー | brew-nix (`telegram`) |
| Text Chat Translator | 翻訳アプリ | App Store → masApps |
| VOCALOID | ボカロエディタ | 独自インストーラー、Nix管理不可 |
| Virtual Desktop Updater | VR 関連 | virtual-desktop-streamer に付属 |
| Yubico Authenticator | YubiKey 認証 | brew-nix (`yubico-authenticator`) |
| zoom.us | Zoom (古い名前) | 既に brew-nix で `zoom` 管理済み |

**管理不要**: Safari, Utilities, Developer (Xcode), Nix Apps, Codex — システムアプリや Nix が自動生成するもの

> DEVELOPER

masで管理されているアプリのリスト出すにはどうしたらいいんだっけ

> AGENT

`mas list` コマンドで Mac App Store 経由でインストールされているアプリの一覧を確認できます。

Mac で以下を実行してください：

```bash
mas list
```

出力形式は以下のようになります：

```
497799835  Xcode (16.2)
409183694  Keynote (14.3)
```

左の数字が App Store ID、右がアプリ名です。この出力を貼ってもらえれば、`homebrew.nix` の `masApps` セクションに追加します。

```nix
masApps = {
  "Xcode" = 497799835;
  "Keynote" = 409183694;
  # ...
};
```

> DEVELOPER

Error: Not installed となるが、listには出てくる

> AGENT

`mas uninstall` は最近のバージョンでは機能しないことがあります。手動で削除してください：

```bash
sudo rm -rf "/Applications/Windows App.app" "/Applications/LimeChat.app" "/Applications/CrystalFetch.app" "/Applications/The Unarchiver.app"
```

もし `/Applications` に見つからない場合は `~/Applications` にある可能性があります：

```bash
ls ~/Applications/ | grep -iE "windows|lime|crystal|unarchiver"
```

```
{"result":"The `ryoppippi/dotfiles` repository organizes its configuration using Nix flakes, `nix-darwin` for macOS, and `home-manager` for both macOS and Linux, with a clear separation of concerns for platform-specific and common configurations.\n\n### 1. Package Separation between Nixpkgs and Homebrew Casks\n\nPackages are primarily managed through Nixpkgs, with Homebrew casks specifically handled for macOS.\nCommon packages are defined in `nix/modules/home/packages.nix` , which includes development tools, search utilities, and cross-platform GUI applications. Platform-conditional packages, like `telegram-desktop`, are also managed here using `lib.optionals` based on `isDarwin` or `isX86Linux` flags .\n\nFor macOS, `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix`  lists macOS-exclusive software from Nixpkgs (e.g., `ghostty-bin`, `cyberduck`)  and Homebrew casks managed via `brew-nix` . Some `brew-nix` packages requiring hash overrides are also defined here . Linux-specific packages are in `nix/modules/linux/packages.nix` .\n\n### 2. Directory Layout for Darwin Modules vs. Home-Manager Modules\n\nThe directory structure separates common Home Manager configurations from platform-specific modules:\n*   **Common Home Manager Modules**: Located in `nix/modules/home/` , these modules contain configurations applicable to both macOS and Linux. Examples include `packages.nix` , `dotfiles.nix` , and `programs/`  for shared program settings.\n*   **Darwin Modules**: Found in `nix/modules/darwin/` , these modules define macOS-specific system configurations using `nix-darwin`. This includes `system.nix`  for system defaults and Homebrew integration, `packages.nix`  for macOS-specific packages, and `dotfiles.nix`  for macOS-specific dotfile symlinks.\n*   **Linux Modules**: Located in `nix/modules/linux/` , these modules contain Linux-specific configurations, such as `packages.nix`  and `dotfiles.nix` .\n\nThe `flake.nix` file  orchestrates the import of these modules, embedding Home Manager within `nix-darwin` for macOS  and using standalone Home Manager for Linux .\n\n### 3. How `brew-nix` Integrates and Where `brewCasks` are Defined\n\n`brew-nix` is integrated as an overlay in the `flake.nix` file . This overlay is conditionally applied only for Darwin systems using `nixpkgs.lib.optionals isDarwin` .\n\n`brewCasks` are defined within `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix` . This file uses `with pkgs.brewCasks;` to list the desired Homebrew cask applications . Additionally, some `brew-nix` packages that require specific hash overrides are also defined here .\n\n### 4. Platform-Specific Package Organization\n\nPlatform-specific packages are organized into dedicated files:\n*   **Common Packages**: `nix/modules/home/packages.nix`  contains packages installed on all platforms. It also includes conditional logic using `lib.optionals (isDarwin || isX86Linux)` for packages like `telegram-desktop` .\n*   **macOS-Specific Packages**: `nix/modules/darwin/packages.nix`  lists packages exclusive to macOS, including those from Nixpkgs and Homebrew casks.\n*   **Linux-Specific Packages**: `nix/modules/linux/packages.nix`  defines packages only for Linux, such as `kanata` and the native `ghostty` package.\n\nPlatform detection is performed using `pkgs.stdenv.isDarwin`, `pkgs.stdenv.isLinux`, and `pkgs.stdenv.hostPlatform.system` .\n\n### 5. Overall Module Colocation Strategy\n\nThe overall module colocation strategy is layered, promoting code reuse while allowing for platform-specific overrides:\n1.  **Common Layer**: Modules in `nix/modules/home/`  define configurations that are shared across all platforms. This includes common packages, dotfiles, and program configurations (e.g., `nix/modules/home/programs/ghostty.nix`  defines shared settings for Ghostty).\n2.  **Platform-Specific Layers**: `nix/modules/darwin/`  and `nix/modules/linux/`  contain modules that handle platform-specific configurations. These modules import the common modules and then apply platform-specific settings or packages. For example, `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix`  configures macOS system defaults and Homebrew, while `nix/modules/linux/default.nix`  includes Linux-specific services like `kanata`.\n3.  **Conditional Imports**: The `flake.nix`  file uses helper functions like `mkLinuxHomeConfig`  and `nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem`  to conditionally import the relevant platform-specific modules and embed Home Manager where appropriate.\n\nThis strategy ensures that common configurations are defined once, while platform differences are managed in dedicated, isolated modules.\n\n## Notes\nThe `README.md`  and `CLAUDE.md`  files provide a high-level overview of the repository's structure and commands, which aligns with the detailed explanation in the \"Platform-Specific Configuration\" wiki page . The `flake.nix`    file is the central point for defining the system's outputs and importing all modules.\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Platform-Specific Configuration (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#2.2)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

{"result":"The repository's Nix configuration is structured to manage system settings and dotfiles across macOS and Linux, with a clear separation of common and platform-specific modules . The `nix/` directory contains the core modular configuration, with subdirectories like `home/`, `darwin/`, and `linux/` organizing platform-specific settings and modules . Profiles and modules are related in that modules define specific configurations, which are then composed into system or home profiles for different platforms  .\n\n## Directory Tree Structure\n\nThe top-level directory structure includes configuration files and directories for various tools and platforms :\n\n```\n.\n├── flake.nix           # Nix entry point\n├── nix/                # Nix configuration modules\n│   ├── modules/        # Core modules\n│   │   ├── home/       # Cross-platform Home Manager modules\n│   │   ├── darwin/     # macOS nix-darwin modules\n│   │   ├── linux/      # Linux Home Manager modules\n│   │   └── lib/        # Helper functions\n│   ├── overlays/       # Nix package overlays\n│   └── packages/       # Node.js package definitions\n├── fish/               # Fish shell config\n├── nvim/               # Neovim config\n├── kanata/             # Kanata keyboard remapper\n├── wezterm/            # Wezterm terminal\n├── agents/skills/      # Shared AI agent skills (Claude, Codex)\n├── claude/             # Claude Code config (user memory)\n└── .claude/rules/      # Path-specific rules\n``` \n\n## Organization under `home/`, `darwin/`, and `linux/`\n\nThe `nix/modules/` directory is central to the configuration, containing subdirectories for `home/`, `darwin/`, and `linux/` .\n\n### `nix/modules/home/`\nThis directory contains modules for Home Manager configurations that are largely cross-platform .\nIt imports common packages, agent skills, Git hooks, program configurations, and dotfile symlinks .\n\nKey modules include:\n*   `agent-skills.nix`: Configures agent skills for tools like Claude Code, managing both external and local skills .\n*   `packages.nix`: Defines a list of common packages installed across platforms, with some conditional inclusions for `telegram-desktop` based on `isDarwin` or `isX86Linux` .\n*   `programs/`: A subdirectory containing configurations for various programs such as `ai-tools.nix`, `fish/`, `claude-code/`, `codex.nix`, `amp.nix`, `gh.nix`, `git/`, `neovim/`, `cursor.nix`, `ghostty.nix`, `bat.nix`, `direnv.nix`, `jj.nix`, `lazygit/`, `opencode/`, and `pip.nix` .\n*   `dotfiles.nix`: Manages common dotfile symlinks using a helper function `mkLinkForce` .\n*   `git-hooks.nix`: Sets up Git hooks for automating tasks like applying Nix changes on commit, checkout, merge, and rebase .\n\n### `nix/modules/darwin/`\nThis directory holds macOS-specific configurations using `nix-darwin` .\nIt includes modules for system settings, packages, and dotfiles specific to macOS .\n\nKey modules include:\n*   `system.nix`: Configures macOS system defaults, security settings (like Touch ID for sudo), Homebrew integration, user shell, and fonts .\n*   `packages.nix`: Defines macOS-exclusive packages from `nixpkgs` and `brew-nix` .\n*   `dotfiles.nix`: Manages macOS-specific dotfile symlinks, including configurations for Karabiner Elements, Xcode key bindings, and Pip .\n\n### `nix/modules/linux/`\nThis directory contains Linux-specific configurations for Home Manager .\nIt imports Linux-specific packages, dotfiles, and services .\n\nKey modules include:\n*   `packages.nix`: Defines Linux-exclusive software like `kanata`, `ghostty`, and `1password` .\n*   `dotfiles.nix`: Handles Linux-specific dotfile symlinks, such as Pip configuration and Ghostty terminal settings .\n*   `services/kanata.nix`: Configures the `kanata` systemd user service for keyboard remapping .\n\n## Relationship between Profiles and Modules\n\nIn this Nix configuration, modules are individual, self-contained units that define specific parts of the system or user configuration . These modules are then composed together to create complete \"profiles\" for different platforms and users .\n\nFor example:\n*   **macOS Profile**: The `darwinConfigurations.${username}` profile is built using `nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem` . This profile imports the macOS-specific `nix/modules/darwin/system.nix` module for system-level settings , and embeds Home Manager modules, including `nix/modules/home/default.nix` and `nix/modules/darwin/default.nix`, for user-space configurations .\n*   **Linux Profiles**: Linux configurations are defined as standalone `homeConfigurations` using the `mkLinuxHomeConfig` helper function . These profiles import `nix/modules/home/default.nix` for common user configurations and `nix/modules/linux/default.nix` for Linux-specific settings .\n\nEssentially, modules provide the building blocks, and profiles are the final assembled configurations tailored for a specific environment (e.g., `aarch64-darwin` or `x86_64-linux`) .\n\n## Notes\nThe `README.md` and `CLAUDE.md` files provide high-level overviews of the repository structure and Nix configuration, which are helpful for understanding the overall organization  . The `Platform-Specific Configuration` wiki page offers a detailed explanation of how modules are composed and how platform differences are handled .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Platform-Specific Configuration (ryoppippi/dotfiles)](/wiki/ryoppippi/dotfiles#2.2)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.com/search/show-me-the-full-directory-tre_77ae58f1-87b0-45ee-bbd9-b5b583dde0c7\n"}
```

ryoppippi/dotfiles と現在の dotnix を比較した結果、以下の問題点が見えます。

## 現状の問題点

### 1. `core/` の命名が不正確
`modules/core/` は実質 **NixOS 専用モジュール** なのに「core（共通基盤）」と名付けている。ryoppippi は `linux/` と命名していて、これが正確。

### 2. プラットフォーム固有ファイルが `home/` に散在
- `home/packages-darwin.nix` (brewCasks含む) → `darwin/` にあるべき
- `home/packages-linux.nix` → `linux/` にあるべき
- `home/darwin.nix`, `home/linux.nix` (薄いラッパー) → 各プラットフォームディレクトリに吸収すべき
- `home/niri.nix`, `home/programs/waybar.nix`, `home/programs/swayidle.nix`, `home/programs/swaylock.nix` → Linux 専用なのに `home/` にある

### 3. プロファイルのプログラム import が重複
`profiles/darwin.nix` と `profiles/cli.nix` がほぼ同じプログラムリストを持っている（gh, tmux, neovim, bat, btop, fastfetch）。

### 4. Homebrew 設定が2箇所に分散
`darwin/homebrew.nix` にシステム cask、`home/packages-darwin.nix` に brew-nix cask。ryoppippi は `darwin/packages.nix` に nixpkgs も brewCasks も統一している。

## ryoppippi 流の理想構造

```
nix/modules/
├── home/              # クロスプラットフォーム共通のみ
│   ├── default.nix
│   ├── packages.nix   # 共通パッケージ
│   ├── dotfiles.nix
│   ├── agent-skills.nix
│   └── programs/      # 共通プログラムのみ
│       ├── zsh.nix, git.nix, jj.nix, claude-code.nix
│       ├── gh.nix, tmux/, neovim.nix, bat.nix, btop.nix
│       ├── fastfetch/, ghostty/, vscode.nix
│       └── (waybar, swayidle, swaylock は移動)
├── darwin/            # macOS 全部ここ
│   ├── default.nix
│   ├── system.nix
│   ├── user.nix       # home/darwin.nix を吸収
│   ├── packages.nix   # nixpkgs + brewCasks 統合
│   ├── homebrew.nix   # システム統合 cask + masApps
│   └── fonts.nix
├── linux/             # core/ をリネーム + Linux固有home統合
│   ├── default.nix
│   ├── user.nix       # home/linux.nix を吸収
│   ├── packages.nix   # システム + ユーザーパッケージ統合
│   ├── fonts.nix, audio.nix, bluetooth.nix, ...
│   └── programs/      # Linux専用プログラム
│       ├── waybar.nix, swayidle.nix, swaylock.nix
│       └── niri.nix
└── lib/
```
